{"parentUuid":"a9aa7d79-a8b0-4399-9eae-7cc57d997200","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"103cde84-18ec-4036-bbbf-c8f26cc8bae4","agentId":"a9445bd82446e9cd4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013nnN84ZXzj9n9wgtbA9MzQ","type":"tool_result","content":"src/routes/transcribe.test.ts\n---registry---\nsrc/providers/assemblyai.ts:90:// to this long (sequential sync-then-async, not a concurrent race — that\nsrc/lib/google-auth.ts:113:  // earlier would let a concurrent caller arrive between mint-completion\nsrc/lib/google-auth.ts:141:  // Assign before awaiting so concurrent callers see the inflight promise.\n---claudemd---\n# CLAUDE.md\n\nHyperWhisper Cloud — Bun + Hono transcription service on Fly.io. Routes audio across Deepgram / Groq / ElevenLabs / Grok / Azure MAI / Google Chirp with credit accounting via Upstash Redis and the Next.js license API.\n\n## Project map\n\n- `src/routes/transcribe.ts` — main `/transcribe` route, fallback chain, header gates\n- `src/providers/` — per-upstream clients (`deepgram.ts`, `groq.ts`, `elevenlabs.ts`, `xai-stt.ts`, `azure-mai.ts`, `google-chirp.ts`) plus shared `types.ts`, `utils.ts`\n- `src/lib/` — `constants.ts` (size caps + replay limit), `gcs-storage.ts`, `google-auth.ts`, `cost-calculator.ts`, `redis.ts`, `responses.ts`, `logging.ts`\n- `src/middleware/` — `auth.ts`, `credits.ts`, `rate-limit.ts`\n- `references/custom-vocab.md` — Deepgram vocabulary boosting rules\n\n## Deployment environments\n\n| Environment | URL | Fly app | Deployed by |\n|-------------|-----|---------|-------------|\n| Development | `transcribe-dev-v2.hyperwhisper.com` | `hyperwhisper-transcribe-dev` | manual / local (`fly.dev.toml`) |\n| Staging     | `transcribe-staging-v2.hyperwhisper.com` | `hyperwhisper-transcribe-staging` | GitHub Actions pre-prod gate (`fly.staging.toml`) |\n| Production  | `transcribe-prod-v2.hyperwhisper.com` | `hyperwhisper-transcribe` | GitHub Actions (`fly.prod.toml`) |\n\n<important if=\"you need to build, typecheck, or deploy the backend\">\n\n| Command | What it does |\n|---|---|\n| `bun --bun ./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit` | TypeScript typecheck |\n| `fly deploy --config fly.dev.toml` | Deploy to dev (`hyperwhisper-transcribe-dev`) — manual/local |\n| `fly deploy --config fly.staging.toml` | Deploy to staging (`hyperwhisper-transcribe-staging`) — normally CI-only |\n| `fly deploy --config fly.prod.toml` | Deploy to prod (`hyperwhisper-transcribe`) |\n| `fly logs --app hyperwhisper-transcribe-dev` | Tail dev logs |\n| `fly logs --app hyperwhisper-transcribe-staging` | Tail staging logs |\n| `fly logs --app hyperwhisper-transcribe` | Tail prod logs |\n\nIterate locally against dev; CI deploys staging as the pre-prod gate and smoke-tests it before promoting to prod.\n</important>\n\n<important if=\"you are about to set, change, rotate, or deploy Fly secrets / env vars on the staging or production apps, or a deploy is failing with `<VAR> not configured`\">\n\nDon't touch them with `fly secrets set` / `fly secrets deploy`. The Fly secrets on `hyperwhisper-transcribe-staging` and `hyperwhisper-transcribe` (provider API keys, Upstash, Google SA, license API URL, etc.) are **synced automatically from Infisical** — a manual change drifts from the source of truth and gets overwritten on the next sync. To add or rotate a secret, change it in Infisical and re-sync; that stages and deploys it to the Fly apps. If a deploy 500s with `<VAR> not configured`, the secrets are staged-but-not-deployed or out of sync — re-sync from Infisical rather than running `fly secrets deploy` by hand.\n</important>\n\n<important if=\"you are touching Deepgram vocabulary, the `initial_prompt` query param, or `buildInitialTranscriptionPrompt` in any client\">\n\nRead `references/custom-vocab.md`. Nova-3 only supports `keyterm` in monolingual mode; auto-detect mode silently drops `keywords` / `keyterm`. Client-side cap: 100 terms.\n</important>\n\n<important if=\"you are modifying the transcribe route, the request flow, or which clients hit the Fly host\">\n\nClient entry points that terminate at `/transcribe`:\n- macOS: `app/macos/hyperwhisper/Managers/Transcription/Providers/Cloud/HyperWhisperCloudProvider.swift` (`buildInitialTranscriptionPrompt`) and `HyperWhisperRoutedTranscription.swift` (for Azure MAI / Google Chirp)\n- Windows: `app/windows/HyperWhisper/Services/HyperWhisperCloudService.cs` and `HyperWhisperRoutedTranscriptionClient.cs`\n\nChanges to query params (`account_key` / legacy `license_key`, `device_id`, `language`, `initial_prompt`, `mode`), `X-STT-Provider`, response shape, or error codes must land in clients in the same PR cycle.\n\nThe auth credential is accepted under **two param names**: `account_key` (canonical, preferred) and `license_key` (legacy alias). Every entry point (`transcribe`, `assistant`, `post-process`, `usage`, `ws-streaming-deepgram`) reads `account_key` first, then falls back to `license_key`, so installed native apps that still send `license_key` keep working. Both carry the same key string — they're aliases, not different credentials.\n</important>\n\n<important if=\"you are touching google-chirp.ts, gcs-storage.ts, or the GCS scratch bucket\">\n\nSpeech V2 sync `recognize` enforces TWO caps: ≤ 10 MB AND ≤ ~60 s. Both rejections are 400 INVALID_ARGUMENT. `google-chirp.ts` gates inline on both — the duration gate uses `estimateAudioSeconds(byteLength, contentType)` with a 55 s budget. Logs `provider.inline_disqualified_by_duration` when a payload fits bytes but fails duration.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"950b9f1d-2764-4ebb-b5ff-50f8bbf1ee85","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:43:26.745Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"src/routes/transcribe.test.ts\n---registry---\nsrc/providers/assemblyai.ts:90:// to this long (sequential sync-then-async, not a concurrent race — that\nsrc/lib/google-auth.ts:113:  // earlier would let a concurrent caller arrive between mint-completion\nsrc/lib/google-auth.ts:141:  // Assign before awaiting so concurrent callers see the inflight promise.\n---claudemd---\n# CLAUDE.md\n\nHyperWhisper Cloud — Bun + Hono transcription service on Fly.io. Routes audio across Deepgram / Groq / ElevenLabs / Grok / Azure MAI / Google Chirp with credit accounting via Upstash Redis and the Next.js license API.\n\n## Project map\n\n- `src/routes/transcribe.ts` — main `/transcribe` route, fallback chain, header gates\n- `src/providers/` — per-upstream clients (`deepgram.ts`, `groq.ts`, `elevenlabs.ts`, `xai-stt.ts`, `azure-mai.ts`, `google-chirp.ts`) plus shared `types.ts`, `utils.ts`\n- `src/lib/` — `constants.ts` (size caps + replay limit), `gcs-storage.ts`, `google-auth.ts`, `cost-calculator.ts`, `redis.ts`, `responses.ts`, `logging.ts`\n- `src/middleware/` — `auth.ts`, `credits.ts`, `rate-limit.ts`\n- `references/custom-vocab.md` — Deepgram vocabulary boosting rules\n\n## Deployment environments\n\n| Environment | URL | Fly app | Deployed by |\n|-------------|-----|---------|-------------|\n| Development | `transcribe-dev-v2.hyperwhisper.com` | `hyperwhisper-transcribe-dev` | manual / local (`fly.dev.toml`) |\n| Staging     | `transcribe-staging-v2.hyperwhisper.com` | `hyperwhisper-transcribe-staging` | GitHub Actions pre-prod gate (`fly.staging.toml`) |\n| Production  | `transcribe-prod-v2.hyperwhisper.com` | `hyperwhisper-transcribe` | GitHub Actions (`fly.prod.toml`) |\n\n<important if=\"you need to build, typecheck, or deploy the backend\">\n\n| Command | What it does |\n|---|---|\n| `bun --bun ./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit` | TypeScript typecheck |\n| `fly deploy --config fly.dev.toml` | Deploy to dev (`hyperwhisper-transcribe-dev`) — manual/local |\n| `fly deploy --config fly.staging.toml` | Deploy to staging (`hyperwhisper-transcribe-staging`) — normally CI-only |\n| `fly deploy --config fly.prod.toml` | Deploy to prod (`hyperwhisper-transcribe`) |\n| `fly logs --app hyperwhisper-transcribe-dev` | Tail dev logs |\n| `fly logs --app hyperwhisper-transcribe-staging` | Tail staging logs |\n| `fly logs --app hyperwhisper-transcribe` | Tail prod logs |\n\nIterate locally against dev; CI deploys staging as the pre-prod gate and smoke-tests it before promoting to prod.\n</important>\n\n<important if=\"you are about to set, change, rotate, or deploy Fly secrets / env vars on the staging or production apps, or a deploy is failing with `<VAR> not configured`\">\n\nDon't touch them with `fly secrets set` / `fly secrets deploy`. The Fly secrets on `hyperwhisper-transcribe-staging` and `hyperwhisper-transcribe` (provider API keys, Upstash, Google SA, license API URL, etc.) are **synced automatically from Infisical** — a manual change drifts from the source of truth and gets overwritten on the next sync. To add or rotate a secret, change it in Infisical and re-sync; that stages and deploys it to the Fly apps. If a deploy 500s with `<VAR> not configured`, the secrets are staged-but-not-deployed or out of sync — re-sync from Infisical rather than running `fly secrets deploy` by hand.\n</important>\n\n<important if=\"you are touching Deepgram vocabulary, the `initial_prompt` query param, or `buildInitialTranscriptionPrompt` in any client\">\n\nRead `references/custom-vocab.md`. Nova-3 only supports `keyterm` in monolingual mode; auto-detect mode silently drops `keywords` / `keyterm`. Client-side cap: 100 terms.\n</important>\n\n<important if=\"you are modifying the transcribe route, the request flow, or which clients hit the Fly host\">\n\nClient entry points that terminate at `/transcribe`:\n- macOS: `app/macos/hyperwhisper/Managers/Transcription/Providers/Cloud/HyperWhisperCloudProvider.swift` (`buildInitialTranscriptionPrompt`) and `HyperWhisperRoutedTranscription.swift` (for Azure MAI / Google Chirp)\n- Windows: `app/windows/HyperWhisper/Services/HyperWhisperCloudService.cs` and `HyperWhisperRoutedTranscriptionClient.cs`\n\nChanges to query params (`account_key` / legacy `license_key`, `device_id`, `language`, `initial_prompt`, `mode`), `X-STT-Provider`, response shape, or error codes must land in clients in the same PR cycle.\n\nThe auth credential is accepted under **two param names**: `account_key` (canonical, preferred) and `license_key` (legacy alias). Every entry point (`transcribe`, `assistant`, `post-process`, `usage`, `ws-streaming-deepgram`) reads `account_key` first, then falls back to `license_key`, so installed native apps that still send `license_key` keep working. Both carry the same key string — they're aliases, not different credentials.\n</important>\n\n<important if=\"you are touching google-chirp.ts, gcs-storage.ts, or the GCS scratch bucket\">\n\nSpeech V2 sync `recognize` enforces TWO caps: ≤ 10 MB AND ≤ ~60 s. Both rejections are 400 INVALID_ARGUMENT. `google-chirp.ts` gates inline on both — the duration gate uses `estimateAudioSeconds(byteLength, contentType)` with a 55 s budget. Logs `provider.inline_disqualified_by_duration` when a payload fits bytes but fails duration.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a9aa7d79-a8b0-4399-9eae-7cc57d997200","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

error,"}]},"uuid":"f4a7805b-7aad-4bf5-a6a9-8ebe4c03b2c5","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:44:12.369Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/app/macos/hyperwhisper/Managers/AudioRecording/Streaming/StreamingTranscriptionClient.swift","content":"    ///\n    /// DELEGATION TO STRATEGY:\n    /// The strategy's parseMessage() converts provider-specific JSON into normalized\n    /// StreamingProviderEvent values. This method then dispatches each event type\n    /// to the appropriate callback.\n    ///\n    /// EVENT DISPATCH:\n    /// | Event              | Action                                          |\n    /// |-------------------|------------------------------------------------|\n    /// | .sessionStarted   | Store session ID, log                           |\n    /// | .finalTranscript  | Call onTranscriptUpdate with isFinal=true       |\n    /// | .partialTranscript| Call onTranscriptUpdate with isFinal=false      |\n    /// | .sessionComplete  | Call onSessionComplete with duration and credits |\n    /// | .error            | Store error, emit error state, call onError     |\n    /// | .metadata         | Debug log only (not surfaced to UI)             |\n    private func processServerMessage(_ jsonString: String) async {\n        guard let event = strategy.parseMessage(jsonString) else {\n            logger.debug(\"Unhandled message from provider\")\n            return\n        }\n\n        switch event {\n        case .sessionStarted(let id):\n            await MainActor.run {\n                self.sessionId = id ?? \"direct\"\n                self.logger.info(\"Session started: \\(self.sessionId ?? \"unknown\", privacy: .public)\")\n            }\n\n        case .finalTranscript(let text):\n            await MainActor.run {\n                self.onTranscriptUpdate?(text, true)\n            }\n\n        case .finalTranscriptAndSessionComplete(let text, let duration, let credits):\n            await MainActor.run {\n                self.onTranscriptUpdate?(text, true)\n                self.didReceiveSessionComplete = true\n                self.logger.info(\"Session complete: \\(duration, privacy: .public)s, \\(credits, privacy: .public) credits\")\n                self.onSessionComplete?(duration, credits)\n            }\n\n        case .partialTranscript(let text):\n            await MainActor.run {\n                self.onTranscriptUpdate?(text, false)\n            }\n\n        case .sessionComplete(let duration, let credits):\n            await MainActor.run {\n                self.didReceiveSessionComplete = true\n                self.logger.info(\"Session complete: \\(duration, privacy: .public)s, \\(credits, privacy: .public) credits\")\n                self.onSessionComplete?(duration, credits)\n            }\n\n        case .error(let message):\n            await MainActor.run {\n                self.logger.error(\"Provider error: \\(message, privacy: .public)\")\n                let error = StreamingError.serverError(message)\n                self.lastError = error\n                SentryService.capture(\n                    error: error,","numLines":60,"startLine":515,"totalLines":960}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"70639432-50df-428b-9431-ee2b33f72c79","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

The callback fires on the audio\n        // tap thread, so an in-flight invocation can race a reconnect: it would\n        // otherwise read the freshly swapped-in socket and send audio before that\n        // socket's startMessages configure the session. Binding to one socket\n        // generation means a stale closure can only ever hit its own (dead) socket.\n        let connectedSocket = webSocketTask\n        capture.onAudioData = { [weak self] pcmData in\n            guard let self = self, let ws = connectedSocket else { return }\n\n            // Let strategy handle provider-specific periodic tasks (e.g., Deepgram KeepAlive)\n            self.strategy.onAudioSendOpportunity { msg in\n                ws.send(msg) { _ in }\n            }\n\n            // Encode and send the audio chunk\n            let encoded = self.strategy.encodeAudioChunk(pcmData)\n            ws.send(encoded) { _ in }\n        }\n\n        try await capture.start()\n\n        isConnected = true\n        isStreaming = true\n        onConnectionStateChange?(.streaming)\n        logger.info(\"Streaming session started successfully\")\n\n        // Log WebSocket connected for diagnostics\n        SentryService.addBreadcrumb(\n            message: \"Direct WebSocket connected\",\n            category: \"audio.streaming\",\n            data: [\n                \"provider\": strategy.transcriptionProviderLabel,\n                \"model\": config.model ?? \"default\",\n                \"sessionId\": sessionId ?? \"unknown\"\n            ]\n        )\n    }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Race the stop sequence against a bounded timeout. Providers that wait\n        // for an explicit completion event need enough headroom to flush.\n        let stopTimeout = stopSequence.reduce(5.0) { timeout, step in\n            switch step {\n            case .waitForSessionComplete(let seconds):\n                return max(timeout, seconds + 1.0)\n            case .wait(let seconds):\n                return max(timeout, seconds + 1.0)\n            case .sendText, .closeWebSocket:\n                return timeout\n            }\n        }\n        let timeoutTask = Task {\n            try await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: UInt64(stopTimeout * 1_000_000_000))\n            stopTask.cancel()\n            logger.warning(\"Stop sequence timed out after \\(stopTimeout, privacy: .public)s — force-closing WebSocket\")\n            webSocketTask?.cancel(with: .abnormalClosure, reason: nil)\n        }\n\n        try? await stopTask.value\n        timeoutTask.cancel()\n\n        // STEP 3: Clean up\n        webSocketTask = nil\n        receiveTask?.cancel()\n        receiveTask = nil\n        currentConfig = nil\n\n        // Break URLSession → delegate retain cycle\n        urlSession?.invalidateAndCancel()\n        urlSession = nil\n\n        isConnected = false\n        sessionId = nil\n        onConnectionStateChange?(.idle)\n        logger.info(\"Streaming session stopped\")\n    }\n\n=== send audio ===\n505:        case .data(let data):","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"f87faf62-4d14-4857-b819-76da82963eeb","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:44:27.668Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"        // 3. {"parentUuid":"3dc78d02-aec1-4e00-8d73-1379ccc27182","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9445bd82446e9cd4","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWHrLCB8wrYniYt9atN2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## T3 — Deepgram streaming session: seam findings\n\n---\n\n### BUG-1 — Every session bills a 0.1-credit floor, including zero-audio and failed sessions\n\n- **trace**: `onOpen`/`onClose`/upstream-`close` → `endSession` (ws-streaming-deepgram.ts:188) → `creditsForCost` (cost-calculator.ts:496) → `deductCredits` (credits.ts:118) → `performDeduction` (credits.ts:138) → `POST /api/license/credits`\n- **seam**: `endSession`'s `if (creditsUsed > 0)` gate (:208) assumes `creditsForCost(0)` returns `0`, but `cost-calculator.ts:497` guarantees the opposite — `costUsd <= 0` returns a hard-coded `0.1` floor. The sibling that *does* return 0 (`estimateCreditsForCost`, :505) exists but is not the one called.\n- **verified empirically**: `computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(0) = 0`, `creditsForCost(0) = 0.1`, `estimateCreditsForCost(0) = 0`.\n- **trigger_sketch**: `GET wss://…/ws/streaming-deepgram?account_key=K` → server sends `ready` → client closes with zero binary frames. `onClose` → `endSession` → `totalDurationSeconds = 0` → `creditsUsed = 0.1 > 0` → `deductCredits(auth, 0, {audio_duration_seconds: 0, transcription_cost_usd: 0})` → `performDeduction` recomputes `creditsForCost(0) = 0.1`, passes its own `<= 0` guard, and POSTs `amount: 0.1` (which passes `validateCreditDeductionAmount`, 1 dp). Same for: missing `DEEPGRAM_API_KEY` (:238 closes 1011 → onClose → charge), Deepgram handshake rejection, network flap reconnects, and Deepgram's ~10s-of-silence auto-close.\n- **consequence**: Overcharge / phantom billing. Users are debited real credits for zero-second sessions and for *server-side failures* (bad API key, Deepgram 4xx). The client is simultaneously told `{\"type\":\"session_complete\",\"duration_seconds\":0,\"credits_used\":0.1}`, which the macOS app surfaces via `onSessionComplete`. With the client's reconnect-per-utterance pattern this is a per-dictation tax independent of audio.\n- **confidence**: high\n\n---\n\n### BUG-2 — Machine recycle / SIGTERM drops the entire charge for every live streaming session\n\n- **trace**: SIGTERM → `gracefulShutdown` (index.ts:85) → `drainPendingDeductions` (credits.ts:106) → `process.exit(0)` (index.ts:99), bypassing `endSession` (:188)\n- **seam**: `gracefulShutdown` assumes \"the only unflushed money is a deduction *already in flight*\" (its comment says exactly that: \"deductions are fired without awaiting **after the response is flushed**\"). The streaming route guarantees no such thing — it is the one route where the charge is not created until the socket closes, so at SIGTERM there is nothing in `inFlightDeductions` for any active session and nothing closes the sockets.\n- **trigger_sketch**: open a session, stream 40 minutes of audio (under the 100 MB / 52 min cap and under the `auth.credits` runtime ceiling), and be live when Fly deploys / scales down / migrates the machine. `drainPendingDeductions` returns 0 immediately, `process.exit(0)` runs within milliseconds, `onClose` never gets an event-loop turn. Attacker-controllable variant: hold sessions open indefinitely — there is **no max session duration and no server idle timeout**, and the 30s `pingInterval` (:287) actively defeats Fly's 60s idle timeout — then wait for any deploy.\n- **consequence**: Free service / revenue loss. The user keeps every transcript (relayed live, frame by frame) and is charged nothing. Every production deploy silently zeroes the bill for all concurrent streamers.\n- **confidence**: high\n\n---\n\n### BUG-3 — The backpressure guard is dead code on Bun: `bufferedAmount` is always 0\n\n- **trace**: EP-4 `onMessage(ArrayBuffer)` → `deepgramWs.bufferedAmount > MAX_DEEPGRAM_BUFFERED_BYTES` (:326) → `deepgramWs.send(data)` (:333)\n- **seam**: :326 assumes the WHATWG `bufferedAmount` semantic (bytes queued but not yet written); Bun's client `WebSocket` does not implement it — the property exists and reads `0` forever.\n- **verified empirically** (Bun 1.3.11, this machine): connected a client WS to a local Bun server and synchronously pushed 4,000 × 64 KB (256 MB) with no event-loop turn for the peer to drain — `max bufferedAmount observed = 0`.\n- **trigger_sketch**: client opens a session and blasts 1 MB binary frames as fast as the socket accepts them (32× realtime is enough). The guard never fires, so every frame goes into Bun's internal send queue. The only real bound is `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES = 100 MB` **per session**, and there is no per-license-key session limit anywhere in `src/` — so N sockets on one key ⇒ N × 100 MB of queued audio on a Fly machine.\n- **consequence**: The exact OOM the file's own comment (:58-70) says it prevents (\"Without these caps the client can grow the outbound socket's buffer unbounded until the Fly machine OOMs\") is still reachable; only the cumulative cap actually bounds it. Also means the `'Transcription service busy, audio dropped'` error path is unreachable, so genuine congestion silently backs up instead of degrading.\n- **confidence**: high (behaviour empirically confirmed; I did not read Bun's WS internals to prove the queue itself is unbounded — see depth limits)\n\n---\n\n### BUG-4 — Concurrent sessions all authorize against the same stale `auth.credits`; overspend scales linearly with socket count\n\n- **trace**: EP-9 `wsStreamingPreflight` → `validateAuth` (auth.ts:96, Redis cache hit) → `validateCredits` (credits.ts:41) → EP-4 runtime ceiling `creditsUsed >= auth.credits` (:339)\n- **seam**: the runtime ceiling at :339 assumes `auth.credits` is *this session's* remaining budget; `validateAuth` only guarantees \"the balance the licensing API reported the last time anyone asked, up to `LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600` ago\" — there is no reservation, no per-key session registry, and no re-read.\n- **trigger_sketch**: buy the minimum passing balance (`minimumStreamingCredits() = 2.8` credits ≈ 30 s of Nova-3). Open 100 sockets with the same `account_key` within the cache TTL. Each preflight sees `balance 2.8 ≥ 2.8` → passes. Each session independently streams until *its own* `totalDurationSeconds` reaches 2.8 credits. Total delivered ≈ 100 × 30 s = 50 minutes; total debited ≤ 2.8 credits (the DB decrement is grant-based and floors at 0 per the route's own comment, so the 99 excess POSTs simply hit an empty balance). Sequential variant: reconnect faster than the deduction POST round-trip to Vercel — the cache write-back in `recordLicenseUsage` (credits.ts:86) lands after the next handshake has already read the old value.\n- **consequence**: Security/billing bypass — unbounded free transcription, multiplier = number of parallel sockets. Compounded by the same POST's lost-update race: two concurrent deductions write `credits_remaining` back to the same Redis key, so the *later-landing* (higher) value can resurrect a balance the DB has already spent, and each write refreshes the 1 h TTL.\n- **confidence**: high\n\n---\n\n### BUG-5 — Deduction failures are swallowed with no retry; the whole session's charge is silently lost\n\n- **trace**: `endSession` (:209 `.catch(console.error)`) → `deductCredits` (credits.ts:118) → `recordLicenseUsage` (credits.ts:53) → `catch { console.warn }` (credits.ts:74-96)\n- **seam**: `endSession` treats \"the deduction promise was created\" as \"the user was charged\"; `recordLicenseUsage` guarantees only \"we attempted one fire-and-forget POST with a 10 s timeout and will never mention it again\". There is no queue, no retry (`retryWithBackoff` exists in `lib/utils.ts` and is not used here), and no dead-letter.\n- **trigger_sketch**: end a 45-minute session (≈ 250 credits) while `www.hyperwhisper.com` is cold-starting or mid-deploy → non-`ok` response or `AbortSignal.timeout(10_000)` fires → `console.warn` → charge gone. Deterministic variant: a license refunded/revoked mid-session returns `status !== \"granted\"` → 400 → warn → the session that already streamed 45 min is free.\n- **consequence**: Free service; billing fails open on exactly the path where the amount is largest. `drainPendingDeductions` makes this worse-looking than it is — it counts and logs `machine.shutdown_drained_deductions` from `pendingCount` regardless of whether the settled promises actually succeeded (credits.ts:115 returns `pendingCount` even when the `timeout` leg of the `Promise.race` won).\n- **confidence**: medium-high\n\n---\n\n### BUG-6 — Audio sent before the Deepgram handshake completes is silently discarded, and bypasses the cumulative byte accounting\n\n- **trace**: EP-4 `onMessage` guard `!deepgramWs || deepgramWs.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN` (:294) → returns *before* `bytesReceived += data.byteLength` (:303)\n- **seam**: the comment at :300 states the invariant \"Count **every** inbound frame toward the session total first — even ones we reject below\"; the guard three lines above breaks it for the entire upstream-CONNECTING window. Separately, the route's client contract assumes the client waits for `{\"type\":\"ready\"}` (:253) — the macOS client does not: `StreamingTranscriptionClient.swift:310` wires `capture.onAudioData → ws.send(...)` at socket-open time, and `.sessionStarted` (:537) only stores the session id.\n- **trigger_sketch**: hotkey-down → client WS upgrade completes (101 sent before `onOpen` runs) → user starts speaking → server is still doing TLS+WS handshake to `api.deepgram.com` (typ. 50-300 ms from Fly) → every PCM frame in that window hits :294 and is dropped with no error frame and no counter increment. Abuse variant: open the socket and flood 1 MB frames during the handshake window (and again after the upstream closes but before the client socket does) — unlimited bytes, none counted toward `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES`.\n- **consequence**: (a) silent data loss — the head of every utterance (~1.6-10 KB of PCM) is missing from the transcript with no user-visible signal; (b) the cumulative cap's stated \"reliably closes the connection under a flood\" property does not hold for frames in that window.\n- **confidence**: medium-high for the data loss (client verified not to gate on `ready`), medium for the accounting bypass\n\n---\n\n### BUG-7 — `language` is unvalidated and unmappable; a rejected code produces an opaque error plus a phantom charge, with no failover\n\n- **trace**: EP-9 preflight (no validation) → `buildDeepgramUrl` (:108-110) → upstream `error` listener (:275) → upstream `close` → `endSession` (:188)\n- **seam**: the streaming route assumes any lower-cased `language` is acceptable to `model=nova-3`; the REST sibling `providers/deepgram.ts:138` explicitly documents the opposite (\"Other 4xx (e.g. **400 on an unaccepted language code/format**)\") and handles it by failing over to another provider. The WS path has no allowlist, no model remap, and no failover.\n- **trigger_sketch**: `?account_key=K&language=es` (nova-3 accepts `en` / `multi`). Deepgram rejects the handshake → `error` listener sends `{\"type\":\"error\",\"message\":\"Transcription service error\"}` → `close` → `endSession` bills the BUG-1 floor of 0.1 credits.\n- **consequence**: A whole language class of users gets a generic, non-actionable error and is charged 0.1 credits per retry, while the same language on `/transcribe` transparently falls over to a sibling provider (divergent behaviour between the two paths).\n- **confidence**: medium\n- **Explicitly checked and CLEAR — no injection**: both `language` (`params.set`) and `vocabulary` terms (`params.append`) go through `URLSearchParams`, which percent-encodes `&`, `=` and `?`. Confirmed by running the real builder shape: `language=en&model=whisper-large&x=` serializes to `language=en%26model%3Dwhisper-large%26x%3D`. `model=`/`tier=` **cannot** be added or overridden, so no \"pick a more expensive model\" attack. (Note `vocabulary` is silently ignored whenever `language` is absent or `auto` — that is deliberate per `CLAUDE.md`/`references/custom-vocab.md` and the client mirrors it, so not a bug.)\n\n---\n\n### BUG-8 — Upstream `error` neither ends the session nor closes anything; a session can hang with the ping interval and upstream socket alive\n\n- **trace**: `deepgramWs` `error` listener (:275-277) → *no* `endSession`, *no* `closeUpstream`, *no* `ws.close`\n- **seam**: the `error` handler assumes a `close` event always follows (which is the only thing that clears `pingInterval`, closes the client socket, and bills). Bun's `WebSocket` guarantees that for handshake failures, but not for every error surface (e.g. an error raised on an already-open socket that then stalls).\n- **trigger_sketch**: upstream errors post-open without a subsequent close → client receives `{\"type\":\"error\"}` but its socket stays open; `onMessage` silently drops all further audio at :294; the 30 s ping keeps the connection alive against Fly's idle timeout indefinitely.\n- **consequence**: hang — a permanently wedged session holding a machine slot, a leaked interval, and a leaked upstream socket, with zero billing. This is the only path where `pingInterval` (cleared only inside `endSession`, :192) can outlive the session.\n- **confidence**: low-medium (depends on Bun's exact error/close pairing, which I did not confirm in source)\n\n---\n\n### BUG-9 — Error contract: swallowed upstream parse failures and unreadable preflight rejections\n\n- **trace**: `deepgramWs` `message` listener → `JSON.parse` in try/catch → `console.warn` only (:270-272); and `wsStreamingPreflight` → `return creditCheck.response` (:161)\n- **seam**: :257 assumes `event.data` is always a JSON string; Bun delivers `Buffer`/`ArrayBuffer` for binary frames, and Deepgram can emit non-`Results` shapes. The client is told nothing on failure. Separately, `validateCredits` returns an **HTTP** `402`-style JSON `Response` for a request that is a **WebSocket upgrade** — a WS client cannot read the body of a failed upgrade.\n- **trigger_sketch**: a malformed/binary upstream frame → the transcript segment vanishes with no `error` message to the client; a user with 2.7 credits opening a session → generic \"connection failed\" instead of \"insufficient credits\".\n- **consequence**: silent transcript gaps (wrong data, no signal) and a misleading connection error that the app cannot map to a top-up prompt.\n- **confidence**: medium (parse swallow), low (402-on-upgrade)\n\n---\n\n### `sessionEnded` guard — checked, it is CORRECT\n\nEP-25/26/27 all race into `endSession`, but `if (sessionEnded) return; sessionEnded = true;` (:189-190) executes **synchronously before any await**, and JS single-threading means no interleaving between the check and the set. **Double commit is impossible.** *Zero* commit is possible only via the process-death path (BUG-2) and the swallowed-POST path (BUG-5), not via the guard. Similarly, `pingInterval` is assigned in the same synchronous block that registers the upstream listeners, so no listener can fire before it exists — the only leak is BUG-8.\n\n---\n\n### depth_limits\n\n- Did not read Bun's `WebSocket` client internals to prove the outbound send queue grows unbounded — only proved empirically that `bufferedAmount` never becomes non-zero, which is what makes the guard dead.\n- Did not open `nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts::spendCreditGrantsByProvenance`;
